#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、具身智能、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：HumanoidBench, MBRL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Model-based reinforcement learning has shown superior sample efficiency by leveraging the learned latent dynamics either in the form of derivative-free planning/imagined rollout training or dynamics gradient-based policy updates. While these approaches achieve comparatively fast learning, they all suffer, to varying degrees, from issues of recurrent compounding errors, high inference-time latency, and instability in backward propagation through time (BPTT). In this work, we propose a model-based RL approach that uses the chunk world model as a differentiable training-time evaluator to optimize the chunk actor without recurrent model invocation. Through the collaborative chunk-wise prediction of the world model and policy, we can generate H-step hypothetical rollouts in a single forward pass and directly optimize value expansion, avoiding the costly and unstable BPTT. We evaluate our method on 19 challenging high-dimensional humanoid tasks from HumanoidBench and deploy our method to the real-world for quadruped bipedal walking. Project Website: https://song-wx.github.io/cmpo.github.io/

</details>

- **相关领域**: 强化学习, 具身智能, 机器人, 世界模型

#### 研究背景与动机

《Sparse-TD-JEPA: Improving Zero-Shot Reinforcement Learning With Sparse Latent Task Representations》归入 强化学习、具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：OGBench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Successor-feature-based zero-shot reinforcement learning methods pre-train a task encoder and successor-feature predictor on offline, reward-free data, then recover a policy at test time by solving a closed-form linear regression over a small labeled dataset. We identify an underexplored limitation of this approach: when the task encoder produces dense representations, the inferred reward signal becomes diffuse across the state space, reducing the contrast between goal-relevant states and the background and providing a weaker signal for the policy to follow. We address this limitation by introducing an l1 sparsity penalty that concentrates reward-predictive structure into a smaller effective subspace of the task encoder's latent space. The penalty is applied only during training and requires no architectural change or modification to the closed-form inference procedure. We instantiate this approach as Sparse-TD-JEPA, a single-line of code addition based on Temporal-Difference Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (TD-JEPA) that significantly improved its performance in zero-shot reinforcement learning. We evaluate Sparse-TD-JEPA on 65 tasks across the DeepMind Control (DMC) Suite and OGBench, covering locomotion, navigation, trajectory stitching, puzzle solving, and robotic manipulation. Sparse-TD-JEPA improves over TD-JEPA in all 13 domain-level comparisons. On DMC, it increases the average return from 661 +- 6 to 714 +- 3. On OGBench, it increases the average success rate from 38.0 +- 0.8 to 48.4 +- 2.3, with improvements of 13.4-18.0 percentage points on the evaluated antmaze navigation and stitching domains. Overall, Sparse-TD-JEPA achieves the highest mean performance among established zero-shot baselines in nine of the 13 domains. Analysis further shows that sparsity produces a sharper and more task-informative inferred reward landscape.

</details>

- **作者**: Haoze Li, Peng Zhou, Peiyuan Wu, Weijie Zhao, Yinan Xin
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.LG

#### 研究背景与动机

《VH-GPO: Verified Handoff for LLM Agent Reinforcement Learning》归入 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：ALFWorld, ActGuide-RL, AgentGym, AgentGym-RL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Group-relative reinforcement learning methods such as GRPO train large language model (LLM) agents by comparing rollouts for the same task. When every trajectory in a group fails with the same reward, relative advantages collapse toward zero, leaving no useful learning signal. A frozen teacher can recover from a student-visited state, but letting it complete the task makes the recovered suffix entirely teacher-generated. We ask: after repairing a student failure, when should control return to the student? We propose outcome-verified teacher-student handoff, instantiated in Verified Handoff for Group Policy Optimization (VH-GPO). The teacher provides enough support to reach a state from which the student can succeed, then returns control so that successful experience is again student-generated. Given an environment-verified teacher recovery, VH-GPO selects the earliest independently confirmed handoff point found within a bounded search budget. With Qwen2.5-1.5B, VH-GPO increases success rates over GRPO from 59.38% to 94.79% on ALFWorld and from 71.62% to 77.34% on WebShop. Improvements also hold with the 7B student, for which additional teacher and student output tokens amount to 1.77% and 2.61% of regular student rollout output tokens on ALFWorld and WebShop, respectively. Ablation studies demonstrate the effectiveness of the key components. These results support using verified teacher assistance to repair failures while reinforcing the student's ability to complete tasks independently.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Grid or Nodes? Probing Object Memory in World Models Under Partial Observability》归入 世界模型、机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：MemoBench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

A robot's world model must remember objects that leave the camera's view. FloWM, a learned world model, keeps them in a spatial grid of features that it moves with five built-in velocities. A node memory instead keeps each object's position and velocity as numbers. We compare them in FloWM's own block world, with an oracle-fed Kalman tracker as the node memory. How the grid is read changes its score: a single-cell probe finds 66% of out-of-view blocks, while FloWM's own probe 89%. FloWM also scores 8 points higher in view although no frame is shown, which we attribute to its write-back of its own predictions. Out of view, the tracker reaches FloWM's range in 14KB against 5.6MB, with no probe. On diagonal motion, outside its five velocities, FloWM finds 11% of blocks even after fine-tuning, while the unchanged tracker keeps 93%. Faster blocks defeat both until one more allowed speed takes the tracker to 94%, where FloWM's built-in shifts would need 25 copies of its grid. A learned node memory must still replace the oracles and handle velocity under noise, long gaps, and the bounce.

</details>

![[assets/2610.15368_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2610.15368](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15368)

- **作者**: Yi Ge, Kangrui Ruan, Jifan Chen, Manish Prajapat, Christian Bock, Xiaoyang Wang, Rein Houthooft, Aritra Sengupta, Varun Kumar
- **cs 子类**: cs.LG

#### 研究背景与动机

《ANTE: Anticipate, Then Execute -- Learning Internal World Models for Coding Agents from Verified Beliefs》归入 世界模型、强化学习、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、强化学习、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：ANTE-RL, PatchWorld, SWE-World, Terminal-Bench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Rapid advances in large language models have made software engineering agents capable of tackling complex coding tasks. Within a containerized environment, a coding agent receives tool responses or execution results, and determines its later actions by those earlier results. However, most existing coding agents never look ahead: their policies do not anticipate the consequences of actions, and the terminal reward barely attributes a failure to certain earlier steps, so an ill-judged edit can corrupt the repository state irreversibly before any signal arrives. Recent code world models aim to address these challenges, but they are either trained and then frozen before reinforcement learning (RL), or reduced to an auxiliary next-token loss on the environment's outputs. We present ANTE (Anticipate, Then Execute), an agentic RL framework that verifies the policy's pre-action predictions by real execution and turns them into per-step credit for its actions and trust-or-abstain decisions. With an internal belief stated before each action, Belief-Anchored Policy Optimization (BAPO) turns the settled predictions into per-step advantages by anchoring credit on the policy's own forecasts. To turn the trained forecasts into better actions at inference, thereby facilitating effective test-time scaling, ANTE-TTO (test-time optimization) selects among candidate actions without an external scorer. ANTE raises Qwen3-32B from 25.0% to 57.2% pass@1 on SWE-bench Verified, 6.2% above a matched GRPO baseline trained without the belief state; its world model's accuracy climbs from 44% to 69% during RL, and the learned foresight capability transfers across harnesses and out-of-distribution domains. In summary, we aim to build a code world model that knows what has happened, what holds now, and what the next action will achieve.

</details>

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) learn world models directly in latent space, but unconstrained autoregressive predictors may contract the latent distribution over long rollouts. We introduce Symplectic Hamiltonian JEPA (LeHami), which replaces the standard predictor with an action-conditioned Hamiltonian flow over a structured latent state (q, p) integrated using an exactly symplectic leapfrog step. This construction preserves phase-space volume at every rollout step and rules out uniform contraction, asymptotically stable fixed points, and merging of distinct latent states, independently of the learned parameters or action sequence. We obtain the momentum-like component from temporal derivatives of the learned position-like representation, providing an explicit second-order state for prediction and control. On conservative and actuated visual pendulum environments, LeHami substantially improves long-horizon velocity prediction over an unconstrained JEPA world model and empirically maintains constant latent volume where the baseline contracts. The learned flow also supports accurate integration at unseen sub-frame step sizes and numerical time reversal. These results show how geometric structure can provide inference-time guarantees that are complementary to representation regularization in latent world models.

</details>

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Could model-free reinforcement learning algorithms actually be learning a model of the environment? In this work, we apply counterfactual interventions which hold everything about a state fixed, besides a targeted change, to explore if the value function of a Proximal Policy Optimization agent forms a world model of the Crafter environment. Despite only being trained to optimize reward, we discover that the value function learns concepts such as the rules governing when a diamond is collectible, water and food having an impact on health, heuristics regarding inventory, and more. Finally, we discuss the safety implications of our visualizations and some shortcomings of current reinforcement learning agents that will prevent us from building truly advanced machine intelligence.

</details>

![[assets/2610.14159_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2610.14159](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14159)

- **作者**: Dayou Li, Jionghao Wang, Peihao Wang, Jiuzhou Lei, Hao Wang, Jichen Chai, Hezhen Hu, Minghui Zheng, Zhangyang Wang, Xin Li, Wenping Wang, Zhiwen Fan
- **cs 子类**: cs.CV, cs.RO

- **关联关键词**: Robotics, RL, WorldModel

#### 研究背景与动机

《Disentangling Task-Relevant Latent Dynamics for Reward Modeling》归入 世界模型、具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：MetaWorld, Real-World, UniVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Reward design remains a major bottleneck for reinforcement learning in robotic manipulation. Handcrafted dense rewards can compensate for the limited guidance provided by sparse rewards, but require substantial task-specific reward engineering. Learning rewards from videos provides a scalable, data-driven alternative to manual reward design. However, existing video-based rewards are often computed in entangled visual spaces where task-relevant state changes are confounded with extraneous visual variation. As a result, the learned reward may respond to visual distractors instead of actual task execution. To address this limitation, we propose DiTaR (Disentangling Task-Relevant Latent Dynamics for Reward Modeling), a framework for learning dense rewards from heterogeneous videos. DiTaR learns a continuous task-relevant representation from multi-frame video latents through an asymmetric two-stage procedure that suppresses task-irrelevant visual variation. In the task-relevant latent space, we train a history- and language-conditioned rectified-flow model to capture task dynamics and derive a directional dense reward from temporal changes in dynamics compatibility. Together, these components enable reward modeling directly over task-relevant dynamics rather than generic visual variation. DiTaR substantially improves policy learning on MetaWorld-v3 and ManiSkill over video-based reward baselines, and transfers to real-world offline RL through reward relabeling. Reward analyses further show that DiTaR provides more reliable rewards.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/ChunkAct_Action_Chunking_for_Agentic_Robotics|ChunkAct: Action Chunking for Agentic Robotics]]

![[assets/2610.18874_figure.png|800]]

#### 研究背景与动机

《ChunkAct: Action Chunking for Agentic Robotics》归入 机器人、具身智能、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Agentic robotics places a large language model (LLM) agent in the robot control loop, where it composes perception, motion, and vision-language-action (VLA) tools under the guidance of a memory and thereby generalizes to new tasks without retraining. This generality comes at a steep price in efficiency. Every primitive requires a round of LLM reasoning over the full interaction history, so the cost of an episode grows with the number of primitives. We find this reasoning largely redundant: once a sub-goal is chosen, its primitives follow a routine already prescribed by memory. We present ChunkAct, which introduces action chunking to agentic robotics. Online, a single call launches a chunk of chained primitives, which executes autonomously and returns control only upon failure. Offline, ChunkAct compiles the memory into these chunks, so execution is memory-free at test time and the planner is reserved for decisions that require reasoning. LLM computation therefore scales with the number of decisions rather than the number of primitives. On LIBERO-Pro, ChunkAct improves task success by 5.0 points over the state of the art in agentic robotics while requiring 2.9x fewer LLM calls, 4.4x fewer input tokens, and 2.3x fewer output tokens.

</details>

---

#### 研究背景与动机

《SEAR: Self-Evolving Agentic Robotics》归入 机器人、具身智能、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Frontier language-model agents can now improve robots on their own: they write control code, test it, and keep what works as skills and memories for the next task. We introduce SEAR (Self-Evolving Agentic Robotics), a framework and benchmark built around two questions. Is the agent really learning, or just trying more often? And does what it learns still help on new tasks, in a new simulator, and on a real robot? The framework runs an agent in three modes: it writes code that becomes the policy (code-as-policy), acts live in the control loop (agent-as-policy), or works as a researcher that fine-tunes a vision-language-action model (auto-research). The benchmark adds controls that separate retained experience from repeated attempts, familiar tasks, privileged simulator state, larger budgets, and luck. Across seven frontier language models, 330 simulated and five physical tasks, and over four years of agent runtime, we highlight three findings. First, the learning curve of a robotic agent depends both on the overall time budget and the task difficulty relative to model capability. Harder tasks make longer sessions worthwhile; on easier tasks, re-rolling short attempts does just as well. Second, control code and shared routines transfer across tasks and simulators, while task-specific solutions do not. On a real robot, we observe a performance gap between agent-designed practice and novel human-designed scenes. Third, different models evolve more effectively in different ways. Some do best acting live, others writing code first. And a text-only model keeps pace with a comparable multimodal one: perception helps with error assessment, but agentic reasoning and tool use can matter more. We release the benchmark and codebase to support future research.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《EqC: Equilibrium Energy Control for Introspective Lifelong Robot Learning》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Long-term robot autonomy depends on more than effective action generation: a policy must recognize when its behavior is no longer sufficiently supported and use assistance to improve. We introduce Equilibrium Energy Control (EqC), a framework for introspective lifelong robot learning built around an explicit conditional energy landscape. EqC casts generative action refinement as equilibrium-seeking optimization, while the geometry of the refinement process reveals whether the generated behavior remains consistent with the learned landscape. This competence signal enables Energy-Gated DAgger, where persistent energy-consistency violations trigger expert takeover and the resulting recovery trajectories are aggregated for subsequent policy updates. EqC integrates naturally into both Diffusion Transformer policies and flow-matching-based vision-language-action (VLA) policies. Across simulation and real-world bimanual manipulation, EqC matches or improves control performance and robustness while enabling timely intervention near the policy's competence boundary. A real-world study further shows how these interventions progressively expand the range of situations handled autonomously, providing a practical path from static generative controllers toward self-improving robot systems.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/GreenWAM_A_World_Foundation_Model_for_Robotics|GreenWAM: A World Foundation Model for Robotics]]

![[assets/2610.18774_first_page.png|800]]

#### 研究背景与动机

《GreenWAM: A World Foundation Model for Robotics》归入 机器人、具身智能、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：实时应用对效率提出要求；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：给出系统化方法或工具；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；通过评测分析了方法表现；关注鲁棒性或泛化表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

A robot needs a world model that predicts how its actions will change the scene and a policy that chooses those actions. Models are usually built and evaluated for only one of these roles, and every robot records its actions in its own format. We ask whether one robot world model can serve both roles across different bodies, and what makes this work. We present GreenWAM, a world foundation model for robotics, together with the recipe that produces it: (1) a shared anatomical action space that describes 20 robot families, from bimanual arms to dexterous humanoids, in common joint coordinates; (2) joint training on these families in visual, action-conditioned and world-action modes; and (3) policies that split an action expert off the generator. As a world model, GreenWAM attains top-tier trajectory, depth and 3D accuracy on WorldArena 1.0, ahead of every world-action model and video generator in our comparison. As a policy, it scores among the top entries of the RoboTwin 2.0 leaderboard, reaches the highest zero-shot LIBERO-plus success among the compared methods and is competitive with dedicated vision-language-action and world-action policies on CALVIN and LIBERO. We further adapt GreenWAM to a real humanoid, where it succeeds more often than policies adapted from $π_{0.5}$ and from Cosmos 3 Edge, and distill it into a single-step policy for real-time control. Controlled experiments yield three findings: robot specialization improves world prediction and control together; an action expert helps when split off at the start of adaptation; and keeping world-model modes during policy adaptation preserves robustness to scene changes.

</details>

- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.LG, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 强化学习, 具身智能, 世界模型

- **关联关键词**: Robotics, RL

#### 研究背景与动机

《ACCRUE: Continual Off-Policy Reinforcement Learning for Robot Foundation Models》归入 机器人、强化学习、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、强化学习、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training improves robot foundation models (RFMs) beyond their demonstrations, but real-world deployment demands more: learning new tasks efficiently while retaining previously acquired behaviors. Existing continual RL methods on top of VLAs mitigate forgetting but rely on on-policy updates with limited forward transfer, whereas off-policy methods improve single-task sample efficiency without addressing learner persistence across tasks. We propose ACCRUE, a continual off-policy RL framework that carries lightweight actor and critic networks across tasks, allowing each stage to reuse behavioral and value knowledge acquired through prior RL. The inherited critic adapts only on current-task transitions and guides current-task actor learning, while prior behavior is preserved by distilling the actor toward cached targets on a small number of retained episodes. Fixed visual features keep the inherited learner and its replay compatible with base-policy updates. Across contact-rich assembly sequences in simulation and on real-robot hardware, ACCRUE accelerates subsequent-task learning with near-zero forgetting and significantly reduces real-robot training time by 31.0\% to 68.5\%.

</details>

![[assets/2610.18123_figure.jpg|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2610.18123](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18123)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18123
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/MARTS_A_Modular_Agricultural_Robot_for_Targeted_Spraying_of_Diseased_Tomato_Plants_in_Greenhouses|MARTS: A Modular Agricultural Robot for Targeted Spraying of Diseased Tomato Plants in Greenhouses]]
- **作者**: Rajmeet Singh, Manveen Kaur, Yusuf Adamu, Lochan Kshetrimayum, Irfan Hussain
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 具身智能, 大模型
- **相关性评分**: 1.9（加权：具身智能 0.3，大模型 0.1，机器人 1.5）
- **关联关键词**: LLM, Robotics, ComputerVision

#### 研究背景与动机

《MARTS: A Modular Agricultural Robot for Targeted Spraying of Diseased Tomato Plants in Greenhouses》归入 机器人、具身智能、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Broadcast spraying remains the dominant method of disease control in greenhouse tomato production, yet it treats healthy and diseased tissue alike, exposes workers to hazardous aerosols, and depends on operator skill. This paper presents MARTS (Modular Agricultural Robot for Targeted Spraying), a low-cost robotic platform that closes the loop from plant-level disease detection to leaf-localised spray delivery in commercial greenhouses. MARTS integrates a Clearpath Husky A200 mobile base, a UR5 manipulator, a novel modular spraying end-effector built around a single 12 V micro-diaphragm pump, and a wrist-mounted Intel RealSense D435 RGB-D camera. Disease detection reuses our previously published Tomato Leaf Disease Vision Language Model (TLDVLM; Grounding DINO, SAM-2 and LoRA-tuned BLIP-2, 97.27% accuracy over 10 classes); this work focuses on actuation, control and system integration. A ROS pipeline lifts detections into 3D targets, generates collision-aware nozzle poses at a 120 mm stand-off using fitted leaf normals, and scales pump pulse duration to lesion area. Water-sensitive paper calibration showed a linear pulse-volume relationship (R^2 = 0.994). In MuJoCo simulation, an adaptive base-repositioning strategy achieved 100% coverage of 18 waypoints with a maximum orientation error of 1.1 deg. Deployed at the SILAL commercial greenhouse (Al Ain, UAE) on cherry tomatoes under natural infection, MARTS treated 312 diseased leaves in 12 autonomous trials with 92.4% targeting success, 18.7 mm mean spray-centre error, 6.1% adjacent-leaf overspray, and a 71% reduction in pesticide volume versus broadcast spraying. Mean cycle time was 4.8 s per leaf, and the spraying subsystem costs USD 412, 6-10x lower than comparable systems, showing that accurate leaf-level targeting is achievable on commodity hardware.

</details>

![[assets/2610.17750_figure.jpg|800]]

- **作者**: Shikun Ban, Shohei Nagai, Qingyang Song, Hanxi Wan, Eric Zhao, Thomas Wang, Zackory Erickson
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人

#### 研究背景与动机

《Learning Robot Policies for Whole-Body Mobility Assistance via Dynamic Human Motion Generation》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Robotic assistance for human mobility could benefit care recipients and reduce caregiver burden during physically demanding tasks such as Sit-to-Stand (STS) and Lift-and-Transfer (L&amp;T). Learning robot policies for these whole-body assistive tasks is challenging: real-world data collection for human-robot interaction is costly and safety-critical, making simulation an attractive alternative. Prior work, however, typically treats the human as static or minimally responsive and thus fails to capture the cooperative nature of mobility assistance. We present a simulation-based framework for learning whole-body assistive robot policies. At its core is a human policy trained with an adversarial motion prior that produces realistic care-recipient motion in response to the robot's actions. A human-adaptive scripted controller interacts with this policy to collect diverse robot demonstrations at scale. From these demonstrations, we train 3D diffusion policies that map segmented human point clouds and robot joint positions to whole-body actions for a mobile manipulator, without relying on privileged human state at deployment. The resulting robot policies successfully perform both STS and L&amp;T assistance in simulation. Preliminary experiments further demonstrate sim-to-real transfer for STS after fine-tuning on a small amount of real-world data. Videos are available on our project page: https://rchi-lab.github.io/human-gen-mobility-assistance/

</details>

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

We use a frozen frontier vision-language model, GPT-6-Astra, directly as the robot policy on RoboMME, a benchmark of 16 memory-dependent manipulation tasks spanning temporal, spatial, object, and procedural memory. The model keeps the whole episode in context and outputs every end-effector waypoint and gripper command, with no RoboMME-specific training. On the 800 test episodes it succeeds in 92.1%, above the previously reported human score of 90.5%, which was obtained with an oracle skill interface, and far ahead of the best leaderboard entry (60.8%) and of a hierarchical system that pairs the same model with a RoboMME-trained policy (79.1%). It matches or exceeds the human score on 13 of the 16 tasks. Its 63 failures concentrate in two tasks and are mostly premature completions and mistimed or unintended button presses. These results blur the usual division between long-horizon planning, memory and end-effector control, and raise the question of how much of this stack a single general-purpose model can take over in embodied systems. Project website: https://embodied-astra.github.io. Code, prompts and all episode logs: https://github.com/jackbjed/embodied-astra-robomme.

</details>

- **作者**: Vincent Berenz, Tasha Kim, Bernhard Schölkopf
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 具身智能, 大模型

#### 研究背景与动机

《Declarative Reactive Robot Behaviors from Imperative Language Models》归入 机器人、具身智能、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索；使用优化建模或搜索过程。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Large Language Models (LLMs) are predominantly trained on imperative programming languages, and existing work on LLM-based robot programming reflects this: it focuses on goal-directed tasks expressed as plans. However, many robotic applications require not tasks but behaviors--open-ended, continuous patterns of sensory-motor engagement with no termination condition (e.g., greeting visitors, patrolling an area). Such behaviors are better served by declarative and reactive programming, on which LLMs have virtually no training. We investigate whether this gap can be bridged. Using Playful, a reactive scripting language, we show that an LLM-based coding agent can generate correct declarative and reactive programs for robotic behaviors. These programs are produced from under-specified natural language descriptions, given suitable context and a set of reusable programming guidelines. The guidelines encode design principles that redirect the agent from imperative defaults toward proper reactive patterns. Developed once by an expert, they generalize across robots and behaviors. Starting from only the generic robot operating system API, the agent jointly designs both the imperative component (Python modules for sensing, acting, and evaluating) and the declarative component (the reactive script that composes them into behavior logic). The declarative nature of reactive programming supports both incremental refinement through conversational turns and one-shot generation of complete behaviors.

</details>

- **作者**: Quang-Nam Nguyen, Ludovic Righetti
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 具身智能

#### 研究背景与动机

《Task-Space Motion-Force Nonlinear Model Predictive Control for Precise and Compliant Control under Uncertainties》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

In Nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC), the control law is implicitly determined by the solution of an Optimal Control Problem (OCP). Consequently, OCP design is often tailored to specific platforms and tasks, demanding extensive expert knowledge and tuning effort. To address this, we propose a unified NMPC framework that directly plans and controls the robot joints subject to task-space motion and force requirements and is capable of seamlessly generalizing across multiple robotic platforms and diverse tasks. Furthermore, optimization-based methods often treat interactions as rigid contacts, making the torque solution sensitive to the modeling errors of the robot dynamics and the environment. We study the intrinsic task-space impedance of our proposed controller, its partial contraction for differential stability and differential passivity under unmodeled interactions. As a result, our framework enables closing the force-feedback loop in the high-level planning layer to achieve precise motion-force tracking performance, addressing the long-standing challenge of force-feedback NMPC. Finally, we validate our framework on physical hardware, demonstrating hybrid motion-force control on a redundant manipulator and compliant locomotion on a quadruped, while preserving core NMPC advantages such as hard constraint satisfaction, optimal redundancy exploitation, and real-time planning under unforeseen interactions.

</details>

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Generative policies such as diffusion models provide expressive data-driven priors for robot behavior, yet the situations a robot faces at execution time rarely match its training data and impose constraints and objectives the policy never learned. Existing inference-time guidance methods either rely on gradients that can push samples away from the learned prior, or on sampling procedures that can be inefficient, collapse modes, or average them into out-of-distribution predictions. We introduce MOSAIC, a mode- and support-aware framework for gradient-free inference-time control of robotic diffusion models. MOSAIC treats the initial noise and all stochastic reverse-step perturbations as a joint input to the pretrained denoising process and adapts a distribution over this input using importance weights computed from fully denoised outputs. Physical feasibility and consistency with the learned support are balanced using adaptive penalties, while mode-wise importance-weighted updates preserve modes and avoid destructive averaging. The same formulation can jointly steer multiple independently trained diffusion models through shared coupling costs, enabling coordinated behavior without requiring a joint model. We evaluate MOSAIC on navigation, dexterous grasping, motion planning with unseen geometry, and collaborative box manipulation with independently trained robot priors. Across these settings, MOSAIC adapts and composes frozen diffusion priors under new deployment constraints without retraining.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《LABIT: Long-Horizon Robotic Assembly Benchmark for Industrial Tasks》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：FurnitureBench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Robot learning has undergone significant advancements in the domain of compliant control and robotic assembly. The development of novel methodologies has transcended the capacity to address discrete tasks, such as grasping or inserting, and has evolved to encompass the comprehensive construction of entire assemblies. Conventional benchmarks, which are predominantly focused on single tasks, are no longer adequate to address the complexity and interconnected nature of modern manufacturing processes. Consequently, the necessity for benchmarks to encompass a broader spectrum of assembly and subassembly steps is becoming apparent. In light of these developments, we propose LABIT, an industrial robotic assembly benchmark for long-horizon manipulation. This benchmark encompasses DIN-norm assembly tasks, including screwing, placing, inserting, and snapping, under both high and low tolerance conditions. The successive nature of these assembly steps implies that prior errors can influence subsequent manipulation. Consequently, this benchmark is particularly relevant for assessing the robustness of intelligent robotic systems. The proposed benchmarking protocols and novel performance metric, based on a taxonomy of assembly complexity in conjunction with the comprehensive list of parts and their corresponding CAD models, serve as essential tools for research and development in the field. To provide a baseline, we evaluate the benchmark using traditional force-position control and report reliability and score metrics per primitive, subtask, assembly category, and subassembly. Accompanying data is available at https://irp-hka.github.io/robotic-assembly-benchmark/.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/$Δ$_Flow_Matching_for_Robot_Foundation_Models|$Δ$ Flow Matching for Robot Foundation Models]]

![[assets/2610.15794_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2610.15794](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15794)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15794
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/$Δ$_Flow_Matching_for_Robot_Foundation_Models|$Δ$ Flow Matching for Robot Foundation Models]]
- **作者**: Kuangji Zuo, Tuo An, Xinying Guo, Gen Li, Mengfei Zhao, Jiaqi Bai, Bofan Lyu, Zhuoyuan Yu, Jinghan Yang, Jiayi Li, Jindou Jia, Jianfei Yang
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO

- **相关性评分**: 2.0（加权：具身智能 0.9，机器人 1.1）
- **关联关键词**: Multimodal, Robotics, RL

#### 研究背景与动机

《$Δ$ Flow Matching for Robot Foundation Models》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Flow matching is a common generative recipe for robot foundation models. Vision-language-action models (VLAs) generate action chunks, while world-action models (WAMs) additionally generate future visual states. Both start generation from a fixed Gaussian prior, despite temporal continuity between past and future states and actions. This requires costly training and iterative sampling, especially for WAMs with an additional visual branch. We introduce $Δ$-flow, which constructs the source from recent history with mild noise rather than from a fixed Gaussian prior. This moves the source closer to the target, reducing transport to a small residual $Δ$ that can be learned efficiently and enables single-step inference. We integrate $Δ$-flow into the VLA policy $π_{0.5}$ and the WAM policies ImageWAM and Fast-WAM. On LIBERO, ImageWAM reaches 90% success in one epoch rather than six, and Fast-WAM reaches 95% in two epochs rather than eight. With LoRA fine-tuning, $π_{0.5}$ exceeds its baseline's five-epoch performance after one epoch. Single-step inference gives $1.54\times$, $2.49\times$, and $4.45\times$ speedups on $π_{0.5}$, ImageWAM, and Fast-WAM, respectively. It preserves action smoothness, achieving a $4.5\times$ reduction in action jerk compared with one-step Gaussian sampling. $Δ$-flow reaches state-of-the-art generalization performance on LIBERO-Plus. Action-history sources can be applied to pretrained WAM policies without retraining. Replacing the Gaussian action source at inference largely preserves task success with a single sampling step and can slightly improve it. We further study how action representation and visual information in action learning affect history-to-future prediction in $Δ$-flow. These results show that $Δ$-flow is simple yet effective, improving the efficiency and generalization of robot foundation models.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/SurgiCLAW_An_Interpretable_Atomic-Skill_Agent_for_Precision_Robotic_Micromanipulation|SurgiCLAW: An Interpretable Atomic-Skill Agent for Precision Robotic Micromanipulation]]

《SurgiCLAW: An Interpretable Atomic-Skill Agent for Precision Robotic Micromanipulation》归入 机器人、大模型、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、大模型、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：OpenVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and vision--language--action models have provided robots with new capabilities for long-horizon task understanding and planning. In surgical robotics, however, end-to-end prediction of low-level actions remains limited by insufficient precision, opaque decisions, and scarce data. This paper presents SurgiCLAW, a hierarchical skill-based framework for precision surgical manipulation. SurgiCLAW confines an MLLM to intraoperative visual understanding, structured explanation, and atomic surgical-skill selection, while delegating physical execution to verifiable deterministic control programs. For brain--machine-interface implantation, a representative high-precision surgical task, we construct a state-to-skill invocation dataset and expand it through visual and state perturbations. The model first learns structured skill invocation through supervised fine-tuning and is then optimized using three rewards for action correctness, operation prerequisites, and format validity, improving action selection, operational safety, and output parseability. We evaluate subtask success, offline model comparison, real-robot performance, and ablations. On a fixed test set, SurgiCLAW achieves 94.6% skill-selection accuracy and 93.0% macro-averaged F1 (Macro-F1). Across 50 closed-loop real-robot implantation trials, it achieves an 88.0% task success rate.

</details>

![[assets/2610.15740_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2610.15740](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15740)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15740
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/Multimodal_Sensor-Gated_Mid-Air_Launch_and_Vision-Based_Recovery_of_a_Child_UAV_on_a_Hovering_Mothership_Drone|Multimodal Sensor-Gated Mid-Air Launch and Vision-Based Recovery of a Child UAV on a Hovering Mothership Drone]]
- **作者**: Aashish Sahu, R. Prasanth Kumar
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 大模型, 具身智能

#### 研究背景与动机

《Multimodal Sensor-Gated Mid-Air Launch and Vision-Based Recovery of a Child UAV on a Hovering Mothership Drone》归入 机器人、大模型、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：实时应用对效率提出要求。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：围绕论文提出的建模、算法或系统设计进行实验验证。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、大模型、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Launching a micro unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) from a hovering carrier and recovering it in flight requires reliable confirmation that the child is securely seated before the carrier is allowed to descend. This is challenging because vision provides relative guidance but does not directly indicate load transfer, and a downward-facing camera can lose its fiducial near touchdown. This letter presents a mothership-child quadrotor system in which mission transitions are gated by complementary nonvisual sensing. An upward-facing single-beam LiDAR on the recovery deck provides a binary occupancy state using hysteresis and a debounce interval derived from the child's occluding footprint and crossing speed; real-time kinematic (RTK) positioning conditions the carrier hover; and carrier thrust provides a proprioceptive indication of load transfer. In ten outdoor missions using an F450-class mothership and a Q250-class child with camera-guided recovery, nine completed successfully while RTK-fixed positioning was maintained through release and capture. A complementary MuJoCo study of fully autonomous fiducial-based recovery achieved touchdown 0.077 m from the deck center, while marker detection was lost within the final approximately 0.2 m above the docked configuration. In simulation, carrier thrust changed by -17.0 N at release and +17.3 N at capture, agreeing within 6% with the child's 18.0 N hover thrust. The results support a sensing architecture in which vision guides the approach, while range and proprioceptive information corroborate the final docking decision.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/机器人/RootSLAM_Square_Root_Bundle_Adjustment_for_Visual-Inertial_SLAM|RootSLAM: Square Root Bundle Adjustment for Visual-Inertial SLAM]]

#### 研究背景与动机

《RootSLAM: Square Root Bundle Adjustment for Visual-Inertial SLAM》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；实时应用对效率提出要求。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；给出系统化方法或工具；使用优化建模或搜索过程。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Efficient perception is a prerequisite for the small, low-power devices targeted by robotics, mixed reality, autonomous driving, and drone applications, all of which rely on Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) to estimate their motion within their surroundings. Learning-based SLAM systems achieve state-of-the-art accuracy but are demanding in compute, whereas classical geometry-based methods retain a strong efficiency advantage with little to no loss in accuracy. In this work we present RootSLAM, a fully-featured visual-inertial SLAM system for multi-camera setups built on Basalt. RootSLAM achieves accuracy competitive with the state of the art while running up to 20 times faster, facilitating the deployment of perception systems in resource constrained environments. We adapt the square-root bundle adjustment formulation from structure from motion into the multi-camera visual-inertial SLAM setting through a novel reformulation that leverages the system's pose-graph, letting the global optimizer run in single-precision floating point, thus doubling its performance. We provide a thorough benchmark against the state of the art together with an ablation study of our contributions. Our new implementation, including its map management and loop detection module, pose-graph optimizer, and global mapping components, will be open-sourced.

</details>

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、世界模型、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Autoregressive robot video world models based on discrete visual tokens remain unreliable over long rollout horizons. Retaining the entire generated history does not necessarily improve prediction quality. We investigate a possible memory-role conflict in Full Context decoding: persistent scene information, recent visual-action states, and stale generated history compete for attention despite their different predictive roles. This perspective motivates ReCAP (Receding Context with Anchor Preservation), a training-free context-scheduling method for long-horizon robot video world models. ReCAP keeps the pretrained tokenizer, world model, and visual decoder frozen. It preserves the initial scene anchor and a contiguous window of recent complete visual-action blocks while evicting older generated history. The schedule preserves within-block structure and temporal order, requires no additional modules or parameter updates, and bounds active-context growth independently of rollout length. We evaluate ReCAP on RT-1, BridgeV2, LIBERO-90, and CALVIN using frozen autoregressive world models. Across evaluated environments and horizons, ReCAP improves visual fidelity, action-conditioned consistency, and robot structure preservation relative to Full Context. On BridgeV2, it reduces LPIPS by 63.3%. In the system benchmark at H=64, it reduces the logical KV footprint by 73.3% and p50 latency by 6.51%. Attention analyses show increased attention to retained recent states, while history interventions associate larger amounts of stale context with higher prediction error. These observations are consistent with the memory-role-conflict hypothesis, although they do not isolate it from context-length and position-handling effects. Our results support inference-time context scheduling as a practical approach to improving long-horizon robot video prediction and computational efficiency without retraining.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Memory_Without_Curation_Learning_Multi-Stream_Latent_Memory_for_Robot_Policies|Memory Without Curation: Learning Multi-Stream Latent Memory for Robot Policies]]

![[assets/2610.14406_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2610.14406](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14406)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14406
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Memory_Without_Curation_Learning_Multi-Stream_Latent_Memory_for_Robot_Policies|Memory Without Curation: Learning Multi-Stream Latent Memory for Robot Policies]]
- **作者**: Yilin Wu, Rutav Shah, Shuaiyi Huang, Sumedh A. Sontakke, Homanga Bharadhwaj, Andrea Bajcsy, Max Simchowitz, Guanya Shi
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.LG, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 具身智能

- **关联关键词**: Multimodal, Robotics, RL

#### 研究背景与动机

《Memory Without Curation: Learning Multi-Stream Latent Memory for Robot Policies》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：RMBench, ReMemBench, Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

It is widely appreciated that robotic policies require long-term memory to perform complex tasks. However, directly conditioning policies on all past observations leaves policy learning susceptible to learning spurious shortcuts, and introduces large latency at policy deployment. As such, today's memory-augmented policies curate what to remember, either by retaining only recent frames, selected keyframes, or textual summaries, or by learning latent representations of the visual stream alone. In this work, we propose an alternative approach IMPRINT, a policy-agnostic latent memory framework that augments vision-language-action (VLA) models with multi-stream memory without explicitly constraining what the policy should remember. IMPRINT compresses different streams into a latent memory that is updated incrementally at inference, enabling low latency. To avoid shortcut learning, IMPRINT adopts a two-stage training procedure, first supervising memory to reconstruct multiple streams (observations, actions, proprioception) of past information, as well as estimate current task progress, followed by end-to-end finetuning, jointly with the policy, on action prediction. Across both simulation and real-world tasks demanding memory, IMPRINT improves average success over the base VLA by at least 26%, outperforming popular alternatives, and maintaining lowest inference latency among memory-augmented VLAs. Through careful ablations, we demonstrate the utility of incorporating diverse information streams into robotic memory, validating the advantages of foregoing history curation. Project website: https://yilin-wu98.github.io/imprint_website/.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Learning to See through Action: Predictive Curiosity for Robots》归入 机器人、具身智能、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：CRL, PlaNet。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Humans and animals do not learn vision from a fixed set of data: they move, interact, and learn from the visual consequences of their actions. Motivated by this developmental view, we study whether robots can improve visual learning by actively collecting experience through curiosity-driven interaction. We introduce DySCOVER, a self-supervised framework for active robot visual learning. A robot observes RGB frames, learns a visual autoencoder that encodes and reconstructs them, and trains a recurrent world model to predict the next visual latent state conditioned on the current state and action. The agent's policy is trained only with intrinsic rewards: reconstruction error encourages visually novel observations, while the world model's error encourages transitions whose consequences are not yet predictable. This prediction error biases the policy toward more intricate trajectories that enrich the agent's set of experiences with observations beyond those encouraged by visual novelty alone. Our experiments conducted with robotic arms as the embodied agents show that adding predictive curiosity improves the vision model over using visual curiosity alone. Across variations in initial conditions, experience capacity, embodiment, and scene content, adding predictive curiosity consistently improved visual learning. These results show that predictive curiosity can improve active visual learning beyond reconstruction-based curiosity alone.

</details>

- **作者**: Manav Vora, Gokul Puthumanaillam, Aditya Penumarti, Jane Shin, Melkior Ornik
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.RO

#### 研究背景与动机

《Akule: Fast and Scalable Multi-Robot Motion Planning via Sparse Interaction Diffusion》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Diffusion models provide expressive trajectory priors for multi-robot motion planning (MRMP), but scaling them to large teams requires coordinating individual motions without expensive joint computation. We introduce Akule, a sparse interaction diffusion framework that augments a frozen single-robot prior with three learned modules: a reusable pairwise correction model $R_φ$, a contextual selector $U_ψ$ that determines which corrections to evaluate, and a composer $G_ω$ that combines their effects through context-dependent signed weights. This decomposition separates which interactions merit computation from how their effects should be combined into coordinated denoising updates. We train $R_φ$ using matched pairwise trajectory optimizations, then learn $G_ω$ and $U_ψ$ from physically optimized joint trajectories without requiring a prescribed ground-truth interaction graph. The resulting joint proposals are passed to a downstream search-based planner for validation and repair. Across diverse MRMP benchmarks, Akule achieves competitive planning success, complete-planner speedups of up to $25\times$, zero-shot population transfer, and successful planning with up to $256$ robots. Ablations further demonstrate that learned selection and composition reduce pre-repair conflicts and downstream repair effort relative to heuristic selection and uniform summation.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《SenseWeather: Multimodal Sensing for Robust Robotic Perception under Extreme Conditions》归入 机器人、大模型、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；设计端到端框架；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、大模型、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：AirSim, UNet。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Robotic perception must remain reliable when extreme environmental conditions severely degrade the primary RGB observation. Although robotic platforms commonly carry complementary sensors such as depth and infrared, much of the modern perception stack remains RGB-centric, creating a mismatch between multimodal onboard sensing and pretrained downstream models. We study whether complementary sensing can be used upstream to recover a task-compatible RGB observation that preserves existing perception capabilities without modifying or retraining the downstream stack. To this end, we introduce SenseWeather, a controlled multimodal benchmark for robotic perception under severe rain, snow, dust, fog, and darkness, with registered RGB, depth, and infrared observations, clean references, and grounded high-level perception tasks. We further develop Fusion-to-RGB, with two parameter-efficient sensor-fusion realizations based on token-space fusion for a diffusion transformer and residual latent conditioning for an inpainting UNet. Across frozen open-vocabulary grounding and vision-language models, RGB+D+IR fusion improves all paired downstream comparisons over the corresponding RGB-only restorers, including AP50 gains across all ten detector comparisons. Importantly, these perception gains do not consistently follow conventional image-fidelity metrics, and increasing weather severity does not induce monotonic downstream degradation. These results show that complementary sensing can preserve reusable robotic perception under extreme conditions, while highlighting task-level perception robustness as an objective distinct from visual restoration quality.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/Reinforcement_Learning_from_Real-Robot_Interaction_without_High-Quality_State_Estimates|Reinforcement Learning from Real-Robot Interaction without High-Quality State Estimates]]

#### 研究背景与动机

《Reinforcement Learning from Real-Robot Interaction without High-Quality State Estimates》归入 机器人、强化学习、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、强化学习、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：DVRL, HIL-SERL, PlaNet, SERL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

A longstanding goal for robot reinforcement learning (RL) is that robots learn from their own physical experience, as humans and animals do. A key obstacle to learning directly from physical experience, especially outside instrumented lab environments, is that the state of the world is usually unobservable to the learner and must be estimated from on-board sensing. Errors in that estimate corrupt both the policy input and potentially the reward signal. We study this challenge using the contact-rich task of a bipedal robot learning to score soccer goals. We estimate all key state variables on-board with an unscented Kalman filter (UKF) and no external instrumentation (e.g., motion capture or overhead cameras). Because the UKF may incorrectly estimate state when the ball is unobserved, we learn a new state representation with a recurrent state-space model (RSSM) that, critically, only corrects the RSSM's latent state when the ball is observed. Building on RLPD, we introduce RSSM-RLPD and apply it to the task of a NAO robot learning to dribble a soccer ball into a goal. We show that the robot learns to score from multiple starting positions after 30 minutes of real-world interaction, reaching 100% success against 0% for a feed-forward policy under the same training budget. We complement our physical robot experiments with a simulation study and show that RSSM-RLPD learns policies that achieve a higher success rate than behavior cloning, a feed-forward, and GRU baseline. Taken together, our results show that the addition of a learned state representation to existing on-robot learning methods enables RL from real-robot data even with a high degree of partial observability.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/RAIN_Robotic_Task_Generalization_with_Region-Aware_Interaction_Networks|RAIN: Robotic Task Generalization with Region-Aware Interaction Networks]]

![[assets/2610.14039_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2610.14039](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14039)

- **作者**: Seunghun Lee, Kyuhwan Shim, Yeogyeong Song, Seung Hwan Kim, Sihaeng Lee, Byoung-Tak Zhang, Sunghoon Im, Gunhee Lee
- **cs 子类**: cs.CV, cs.RO

#### 研究背景与动机

《RAIN: Robotic Task Generalization with Region-Aware Interaction Networks》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：OpenVLA, Real-World, URL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Existing vision-language-action (VLA) models benefit from large-scale vision-language pretraining, but tend to memorize training trajectories, limiting their ability to adapt learned interactions to new task and scene configurations. To address this problem, we propose \textbf{Region-Aware Interaction Networks (RAIN)}, which formulates a task as a sequence of goal-conditioned subtasks, each specified by an action type and a target-region mask. This formulation allows learned skills to be reused across different objects and scene layouts and composed into task sequences not seen during training. Its Target-adaptive Cross-View Encoder (TCE) conditions multi-view features on target masks and exchanges information across views, while PlanDiT predicts target-directed trajectories and actions. A Transition Head estimates subtask completion to enable automatic transitions. We also introduce a \textbf{Reference-based Retargeting Strategy (RRS)}, which uses recorded trajectories to synthesize smooth approaches to reference interactions with alternative target regions, without collecting additional data. Experiments on \textbf{LIBERO-Analogy}, our new benchmark for robotic task generalization, and real-world tabletop tasks demonstrate that RAIN generalizes more robustly than existing VLA baselines.

</details>

---
